\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell\left(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathsf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&-\frac{T}{2}\log\left(|\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}|\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathsf{y}_{t}-\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\left(\mathsf{y}_{t}-\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&-\frac{T}{2}\left(\log\left(|\mathbf{K}|^{p_{1}}|\mathbf{H}|^{p_{2}}\right)\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\otimes\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&-\frac{T}{2}\left(\log\left(|\mathbf{K}|^{p_{1}}|\mathbf{H}|^{p_{2}}\right)\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&-\frac{T}{2}\left(\log\left(|\mathbf{K}|^{p_{1}}|\mathbf{H}|^{p_{2}}\right)\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right].\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell\left(\mathsf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&-\frac{T-1}{2}\log\left(|\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}|\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=2}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathsf{f}_{t}-\left(\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\right)\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}\right)^{-1}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t}-\left(\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\right)\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&-\frac{T-1}{2}\left(\log\left(|\mathbf{Q}|^{k_{1}}|\mathbf{P}|^{k_{2}}\right)\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=2}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}\right)^{-1}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&-\frac{T-1}{2}\left(\log\left(|\mathbf{Q}|^{k_{1}}|\mathbf{P}|^{k_{2}}\right)\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=2}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{f}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&-\frac{T-1}{2}\left(\log\left(|\mathbf{Q}|^{k_{1}}|\mathbf{P}|^{k_{2}}\right)\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=2}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right].\end{array}

B.2
EM updates

To obtain the EM updates we first compute the derivatives of
\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})
with respect to each parameter in
\bm{\theta}
, and obtain the following

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{R}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{R}}\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)}{\partial\textbf{R}}+\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\right)}{\partial\textbf{R}}-\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{R}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-2\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{C}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{C}}\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)}{\partial\textbf{C}}+\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\right)}{\partial\textbf{C}}-\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{C}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-2\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R^{\prime}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{H}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{H}}\\
&=&-\frac{Tp_{2}}{2}\frac{\partial\log\left(|\mathbf{H}|\right)}{\partial\mathbf{H}}-\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\mathbf{H}}\\
&=&-\frac{Tp_{2}}{2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1},\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{K}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{K}}\\
&=&-\frac{Tp_{1}}{2}\frac{\partial\log\left(|\mathbf{K}|\right)}{\partial\mathbf{K}}-\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\mathbf{K}}\\
&=&-\frac{Tp_{1}}{2}\mathbf{K}^{-1}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1},\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{A}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{A}}\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left(\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{A}}+\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{A}}\right.\\
&&\left.\hskip 216.81pt-\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{A}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}2\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-2\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{B}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{B}}\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left(\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{B}}+\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{B}}\right.\\
&&\hskip 216.81pt\left.-\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\textbf{B}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left(2\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-2\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{P}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{P}}\\
&=&-\frac{(T-1)k_{2}}{2}\frac{\partial\log\left(|\mathbf{P}|\right)}{\partial\mathbf{P}}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\mathbf{P}}\\
&=&-\frac{(T-1)k_{2}}{2}\mathbf{P}^{-1}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1},\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathcal{Q}(\bm{\theta},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial\textbf{Q}}&=&\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{Q}}\\
&=&-\frac{(T-1)k_{1}}{2}\frac{\partial\log\left(|\mathbf{Q}|\right)}{\partial\mathbf{Q}}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)}{\partial\mathbf{Q}}\\
&=&-\frac{(T-1)k_{1}}{2}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}.\end{array}

First order conditions (FOC) then yield

\mathbf{R}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)^{-1},

\mathbf{C}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R^{\prime}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)^{-1},

\mathbf{H}=\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right],

\mathbf{K}=\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right],

\mathbf{A}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)^{-1},

\mathbf{B}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)^{-1},

\mathbf{P}=\frac{1}{(T-1)k_{2}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right],

\mathbf{Q}=\frac{1}{(T-1)k_{1}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right].

Using the conditional moments of the Kalman smoother recursions and (
27
)-(
29
), we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]=\textrm{unvec}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)=\textrm{unvec}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\right)=\mathbf{F}_{t|T},

\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]=\textrm{unvec}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)^{\prime}=\textrm{unvec}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\right)^{\prime}=\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t|T},

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{C^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{C^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{C^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R^{\prime}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{R^{\prime}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{R^{\prime}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{R^{\prime}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)^{\prime}\right)\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)^{\prime}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)^{\prime}\right)\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)^{\prime}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}-\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\\
&&-\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}+\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right],\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\\
&&-\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}+\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right],\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}_{t}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\mathbf{P}^{-1}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\mathbf{P}^{-1}\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\\
&=&\mathbf{P}^{-1}\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t-1|T}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\\
&=&\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t-1|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right),\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\\
&&-\mathbf{A}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&&+\mathbf{A}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{A}^{\prime},\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\\
&&-\mathbf{B}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&&+\mathbf{B}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{B}^{\prime}.\end{array}

Combining these results together with the FOC we obtain

\mathbf{R}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t|T}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{C^{\prime}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}_{t}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\right)^{-1},

\mathbf{C}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{R^{\prime}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)^{-1},

\begin{split}\mathbf{H}=\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}&\left[\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}-\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t|T}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\right.\\
&\left.+\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)^{\prime}\right)\right],\end{split}

\begin{split}\mathbf{K}=\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}&\left[\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}-\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t|T}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right.\\
&\left.+\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)^{\prime}\right)\right],\end{split}

\mathbf{A}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right)\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t-1|T}\right)\right)^{-1},

\mathbf{B}=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t-1|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)^{-1},

\begin{split}\mathbf{P}=\frac{1}{(T-1)k_{2}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}&\left\{\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)-\left[\left(\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right)\right]\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right.-\\
&\left.\mathbf{A}\left[\left(\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right)\right]^{\prime}+\mathbf{A}\left[\left(\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{-1}\mathbf{B}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t-1|T}\right)\right]\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right\},\end{split}

\begin{split}\mathbf{Q}=\frac{1}{(T-1)k_{1}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}&\left\{\mathbf{P}^{-1}\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)-\left[\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right]\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right.\\
&-\mathbf{B}\left[\left(\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right]^{\prime}\\
&+\left.\mathbf{B}\left[\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t-1|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right]\mathbf{B}^{\prime}\right\}.\end{split}

For any iteration
n\geq 0
, given an estimator of the parameters
\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(n)}
, the explicit solutions for
\mathbf{R}
,
\mathbf{C}
,
\mathbf{H}
, and
\mathbf{K}
obtained from the previous FOCs are given in Section
3
. While the solutions for
\mathbf{A}
,
\mathbf{B}
,
\mathbf{P}
, and
\mathbf{Q}
are the following:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}&=&\left(\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right)\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\right)\right)^{-1},\\[18.06749pt]
\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)}&=&\left(\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 72.26999pt\times\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)^{-1},\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(n+1)}&=&\frac{1}{(T-1)k_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n)-1}\star\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 57.81621pt-\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 57.81621pt-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right)^{\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 57.81621pt\left.+\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)\prime}\right],\\[18.06749pt]
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n+1)}&=&\frac{1}{(T-1)k_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(n)-1}\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 57.81621pt-\left(\left(\mathbf{P}^{(n)-1}\mathbf{A}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{\prime}_{(n+1)}\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 57.81621pt-\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)}\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)^{\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 57.81621pt+\left.\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)}\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}\right)\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)\prime}\right].\end{array}

In practice, estimating the factor matrices using

\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(n+1)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(n+1)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(n+1)}

or using those computed directly for the vectorized MAR, i.e.,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)}=\left(\sum^{T}_{t=2}\mathsf{f}^{(n+1)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\left(\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t-1|T}\right)^{-1},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}}^{(n+1)}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(k)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t-1|T}+\bm{\Delta}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}^{(n+1)\prime},

does not make any appreciable difference, so for ease of computation we suggest to use the latter.

B.3
Initial estimators

Let
\mathbf{M}_{1}=(p_{1}p_{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}
and
\mathbf{M}_{2}=(p_{1}p_{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}
, and define
\overline{\mathbf{X}}_{t}=p^{-1}_{2}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{C}}
and
\overline{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}=p^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{R}}
, where
\overline{\mathbf{R}}=\sqrt{p_{1}}\ \bm{\Gamma}^{M_{1}}
and
\overline{\mathbf{C}}=\sqrt{p_{2}}\ \bm{\Gamma}^{M_{2}}
, with
\bm{\Gamma}^{M_{i}}
containing the
k_{i}
leading eigenvectors of
\mathbf{M}_{i}
, for
i=1,2
. The estimators
\overline{\mathbf{R}}
and
\overline{\mathbf{C}}
are called initial estimators are equivalent to those introdcued by
Chen and Fan 2023
. However, a better estimator of the row (column) loadings can be obtained by PC of the data projected onto the space spanned by the column (row) loadings. Specifically, let
\overline{\mathbf{M}}_{1}=(p_{1}p_{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\overline{\mathbf{X}}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{X}}^{\prime}_{t}
and
\overline{\mathbf{M}}_{2}=(p_{1}p_{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\overline{\mathbf{Z}}^{\prime}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}
. Pre-estimators of
\mathbf{R}
and
\mathbf{C}
are given by:

\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}=\sqrt{p_{1}}\ \bm{\Gamma}^{\overline{M}_{1}},\qquad\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}=\sqrt{p_{2}}\ \bm{\Gamma}^{\overline{M}_{2}},

with
\bm{\Gamma}^{\overline{M}_{i}}
containing the
k_{i}
leading eigenvectors of
\overline{\mathbf{M}}_{i}
, for
i=1,2
.

The pre-estimator of the factor matrix is then obtained by linear projection as:

\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)^{\prime}}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}p_{2}}.

Then, letting
\widehat{\mathbf{E}}^{(0)}=\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}
, the pre-estimators of
\mathbf{H}
and
\mathbf{K}
are given by:

\displaystyle[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)}]_{ii}=\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[{\widehat{\mathbf{E}}^{(0)}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}^{(0)\prime}_{t}}\right]_{ii},

\displaystyle\quad[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)}]_{ij}=0,\qquad i,j=1,\ldots,p_{1},\quad i\neq j.

\displaystyle[\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)}]_{ii}=\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[{\widehat{\mathbf{E}}^{(0)\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}^{(0)}_{t}}\right]_{ii},

\displaystyle\quad[\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)}]_{ij}=0,\qquad i,j=1,\ldots,p_{2},\quad i\neq j,

Notice that only the pre-estimators of the diagonal terms are needed for running the EM algorithm.

Then, denoting the pre-estimator of the vectorized factors as
\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}=\frac{(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)})^{\prime}\mathsf{y}_{t}}{p_{1}p_{2}}
, the pre-estimators for the MAR parameters are given by:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{B\otimes\mathbf{A}}}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum^{T}_{t=2}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)\left(\sum^{T}_{t=2}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)^{-1},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum^{T}_{t=2}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{B\otimes\mathbf{A}}}^{(0)}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\right)\left(\sum^{T}_{t=2}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{B\otimes\mathbf{A}}}^{(0)}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime},

Alternatively, we can obtain the pre-estimators
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}
,
\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(0)}
,
\widehat{\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{(0)}
with the projection method of
Chen et al. 2021
computed when using the estimated factors
\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}
.

B.4
EM updates with missing observations

Let
\mathbf{W}_{t}
be a
p_{1}\times p_{2}
matrix with ones corresponding to the non-missing entries in
\mathbf{Y}_{t}
and zeros otherwise. Decomposing
\mathbf{Y}_{t}
as follows

\mathbf{Y}_{t}=\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}+\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t},

we can write

\begin{array}[]{l}\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)\\
=\quad\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}\right)\\
\quad+\;\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}\right)\\
\quad+\;\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}\right)\\
\quad+\;\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}\right).\end{array}

(32)

Moreover, by the law of iterated expectations, we have that

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}&\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]=\\
&\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right].\end{split}

(33)

Using the properties of Hadamard product and (
30
), we obtain

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ(\mathbf{H}^{-1}(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}))\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}\circ(\mathbf{K}^{-1}(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\circ\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right)(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{0}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\circ(\mathbf{K}^{-1}(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\right)(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]=0,\end{array}

(34)

and

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left((\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right))\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)\left((\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime})\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left((\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1})\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\circ(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left((\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1})\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})^{\prime}\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left((\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\right)|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
=\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left((\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\circ(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime})\right)^{\prime}|\mathbf{F}_{t},\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\\
=\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)^{\prime}\right).\\
\end{array}

(35)

Combining (
32
), (
33
), (
34
) and (
35
) we get, up to constant terms, that

\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]&=&-\frac{T}{2}\left(p_{1}\log\left(|\mathbf{K}|\right)+p_{2}\log\left(|\mathbf{H}|\right)\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&&-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}_{t=1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&&-\frac{1}{2}\sum^{T}_{t=1}\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)^{\prime}\right).\end{array}

Therefore, the EM updates must be modified accordingly. We first compute derivatives of
\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]
with respect to
\mathbf{R}
,
\mathbf{C}
,
\mathbf{H}
, and
\mathbf{K}
, obtaining

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{R}}&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\circ\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)\right)}{\partial\textbf{R}}-\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\circ\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{R}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)}{\partial\textbf{R}}-\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{R}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-2\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{C}}&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\circ\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)\right)}{\partial\textbf{R}}-\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\circ\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{C}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)}{\partial\textbf{C}}-\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textbf{tr}\left(\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{C}}\right)\\
&=&\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]-2\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{H}}\\
=-\frac{Tp_{2}}{2}\frac{\partial\log(|\mathbf{H}|)}{\partial\mathbf{H}}-\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]+\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)^{\prime}\right)\right)}{\partial\mathbf{H}}\\
=-\frac{Tp_{2}}{2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\right.\\
\hskip 289.07999pt\left.+\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{H}^{-1},\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lll}\frac{\partial\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\ell(\mathsf{Y}_{T}|\mathbf{F}_{T};\bm{\theta})|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]}{\partial\textbf{K}}\\
=-\frac{Tp_{1}}{2}\frac{\partial\log(|\mathbf{K}|)}{\partial\mathbf{K}}-\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\partial\textbf{tr}\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]+\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)\right)\right)}{\partial\mathbf{H}}\\
=-\frac{Tp_{1}}{2}\mathbf{K}^{-1}+\frac{1}{2}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\right.\\
\hskip 289.07999pt\left.+\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}.\\
\end{array}

The FOC for
\mathbf{R}
then yields

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right],

from which we obtain

\begin{array}[]{rll}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{R}\right),\end{array}

implying that

\begin{array}[]{l}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{R}\right)=\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)^{-1}\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\right).\end{array}

The FOC for
\mathbf{C}
yields

\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]=\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right],\\

from which we obtain

\begin{array}[]{rll}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
&=&\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{C}\right),\end{array}

implying that

\begin{array}[]{l}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{C}\right)=\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)^{-1}\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\right).\end{array}

The FOC for
\mathbf{H}
yields

\begin{array}[]{l}\frac{Tp_{2}}{2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\right.\\
\hskip 289.07999pt\left.+\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{H}^{-1},\\
\end{array}

implying that, for
i=1,\ldots,p_{1}
,

\begin{array}[]{l}[\mathbf{H}]_{ii}=\left[\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}+\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right)}{Tp_{2}}\right]_{ii}.\end{array}

The FOC for
\mathbf{K}
yields

\begin{array}[]{l}\frac{Tp_{1}}{2}\mathbf{K}^{-1}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}\right.\\
\hskip 289.07999pt\left.+\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1},\\
\end{array}

implying that, for
i=1,\ldots,p_{2}
,

\begin{array}[]{l}[\mathbf{K}]_{ii}=\left[\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]^{\prime}+\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\right)}{Tp_{1}}\right]_{ii}.\end{array}

Using the conditional moments computed with the Kalman recursions and (
31
), we obtain

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\otimes\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{1},p_{1}}\right)\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\right)\left(\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\otimes\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{1},p_{1}}\right)\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{1},p_{1}}\right)\left(\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{1},p_{1}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\otimes\left(\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{1},p_{1}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\right)\otimes\left(\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{1},p_{1}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\right)\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\otimes\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{2},p_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\otimes\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{2},p_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{2},p_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\otimes\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{2},p_{2}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\right)\otimes\left(\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{2},p_{2}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)\otimes\left(\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{2},p_{2}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right).\end{array}

Moreover,

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}_{t}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{K}^{-1}_{t}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{K}^{-1}_{t}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{K}^{-1}_{t}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\right)\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{K}^{-1}_{t}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\right),\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{H}^{-1}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{H}^{-1}\star\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{H}^{-1}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\right)\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\mathbf{H}^{-1}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\\
\hskip 86.72377pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\\
\hskip 86.72377pt+\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\\
\hskip 86.72377pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\\
\hskip 86.72377pt+\mathbf{K}^{-1}_{t}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\\
\hskip 86.72377pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\\
\hskip 86.72377pt+\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\theta}}}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)|\mathsf{Y}_{T}\right]\\
\hskip 72.26999pt=\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\\
\hskip 86.72377pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\\
\hskip 86.72377pt+\mathbf{H}^{-1}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\right).\end{array}

Combining these results with the FOC we obtain

\begin{array}[]{lcr}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{R}\right)&=&\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[s,q]}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\right)\otimes\left(\mathbb{I}_{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{1},p_{1}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\right)^{-1}\\
&&\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t|T}\right)\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lcr}\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbf{C}\right)&=&\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{[k,q]}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\star\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\right)\otimes\left(\mathbb{I}_{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}^{(s,q)}_{p_{2},p_{2}}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\right)^{-1}\\
&&\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\textrm{vec}\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)\right),\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\left[\mathbf{H}\right]_{ii}&=&\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right.\right.\\
&&\hskip 72.26999pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\\
&&\hskip 72.26999pt\left.+\mathbf{K}^{-1}_{t}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\left(\mathbf{C}\otimes\mathbf{R}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\right)\right)^{\prime}\\
&&\hskip 72.26999pt\left.+\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right]_{ii},\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\left[\mathbf{K}\right]_{ii}&=&\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)\right.\right.\\
&&\hskip 72.26999pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\\
&&\left.\hskip 72.26999pt+\mathbf{H}^{-1}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t|T}+\bm{\Pi}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\left(\mathbf{R}\otimes\mathbf{C}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\right)\right)^{\prime}\\
&&\left.\hskip 72.26999pt+\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}\right)\right]_{ii}.\end{array}

For any iteration
n\geq 0
, given an estimator of the parameters
\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(n)}
, the explicit solutions for
\mathbf{R}
and
\mathbf{C}
obtained from the previous FOCs are given in Section
5
. While the solutions for the diagonal elements of
\mathbf{H}
and
\mathbf{K}
are the following:

\begin{split}[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n+1)}]_{ii}&=\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right.\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&\hskip 54.2025pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\mathbf{F}^{(n)}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(k+1)\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&\hskip 54.2025pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\mathbf{F}^{(n)}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(k+1)\prime}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&\hskip 54.2025pt\left.+\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n+1)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n+1)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}_{t}}\right)\right)^{\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&\hskip 54.2025pt\left.+\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}^{(n)}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}^{(n)}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right]_{ii},\\[18.06749pt]
[\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n+1)}]_{ii}&=\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n+1)-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\right.\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&\hskip 54.2025pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n+1)-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\mathbf{F}^{(n)}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(k+1)\prime}\right)\right)\\[7.22743pt]
