&\hskip 54.2025pt-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\mathbf{F}^{(n)}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(k+1)\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n+1)-1}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}\circ\mathbf{Y}_{t}\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&\hskip 28.90755pt\left.+\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n+1)-1}\star\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n+1)}\right)\left(\mathbb{K}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\mathbb{K}^{\prime}_{k_{1}k_{2}}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n+1)}\right)^{\prime}\mathbb{D}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}_{t}}\right)\right)^{\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&\hskip 54.2025pt\left.+\left(\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}-\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n+1)-1}\left(\widehat{\textbf{H}}^{(n+1)}\mathbf{1}_{p_{1},p_{2}}\widehat{\textbf{K}}^{(n)}\right)\right]_{ii}.\end{split}

Appendix C
Asymptotic results

As
k_{1}
,
k_{2}
are both fixed constants, without loss of generality, we assume
k_{1}=k_{2}=1
in some parts of the proofs as long as it simplifies the notations.

C.1
Proof of main results

Proof of Proposition
1
.

Recall that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}&=&\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right)^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\mathbf{R}\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right)^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right)^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
\end{array}

we can thus write

\begin{array}[]{rll}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n+1)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}&=&\left\{\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}}\mathbf{\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}C^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left.-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}-\mathbf{C\widehat{J}_{2}}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left.-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left.\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}\right\}\\[7.22743pt]
&&\times\left(\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n)}_{t|T}\right)\right)^{-1}.\end{array}

(36)

Now, let
n=0
, we have

\begin{array}[]{l}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\left(\frac{\mathbf{\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}C^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\leq k^{2}_{2}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}C^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\lesssim\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2})^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 180.67499pt+\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

(37)

by Lemmas
1
,
3
(iii),
3
(v),
8
(iii), and
11
(i),

\begin{array}[]{l}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\left(\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C\widehat{J}_{2}}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\leq k^{2}_{2}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C\widehat{J}_{2}}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\lesssim\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C\widehat{J}_{2}}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 180.67499pt+\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C\widehat{J}_{2}}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

(38)

by Lemmas
1
,
3
(iii),
4
(i),
8
(i), and since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=1}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert&\leq&\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=1}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\right\rVert+2\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=1}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=1}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\
&=&O_{p}(1)\end{array}

by Assumption
(ii)
and Lemma
11
(i),

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right)\right\rVert&\leq&k^{2}_{2}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right\rVert\max\limits_{1\leq t\leq T}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert^{2}}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right\rVert\max\limits_{1\leq t\leq T}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert^{2}}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right\rVert\max\limits_{1\leq t\leq T}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)\end{array}

(39)

by Lemmas
1
,
3
(iii),
4
(ii),
8
(i), and Lemma D.12 in
Barigozzi and Luciani 2024
. Moreover,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert&\lesssim&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

(40)

since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\right\rVert&=&\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}}\left\lVert\left(\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\star\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{e}_{t}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{k_{2}}k^{-1}_{ii}c_{ij}\left[\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{e}_{t}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{(p_{1},k_{1})}_{ij}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\frac{\bar{c}C_{K}}{T\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{k_{2}}\left[\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{e}_{t}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{(p_{1},k_{1})}_{ij}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\bar{c}C_{K}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot i}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemma
11
(iv), and

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert&\leq&\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
(i)
and Lemma
8
(iii),

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}}\right)

by Lemma A.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
. Finally, we have that

\begin{array}[]{l}\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right)-\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\leq\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 180.67499pt+\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 180.67499pt+\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

(41)

since the first addendum is dominated by the other two terms and

\begin{array}[]{l}\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\leq k^{2}_{2}\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\lesssim\left\lVert\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}-\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}\right)\right\rVert+\max\limits_{1\leq t\leq T}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\lesssim\left\lVert\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{f}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right)\right\rVert+\max\limits_{1\leq t\leq T}\left\lVert\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)\end{array}

by Lemmas
1
,
3
(iii),
3
(v),
11
(i) and Lemma D.12 in
Barigozzi and Luciani 2024
,

\begin{array}[]{l}\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\leq k^{2}_{2}\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt\lesssim\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2})^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 144.54pt=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
(ii)
and Lemmas
1
,
3
(iii),
3
(v),
4
(ii) and
8
(iii). Moreover,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}_{t=1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\right)\right)^{-1}\right\rVert&=&\left\lVert\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}(1)\end{array}

(42)

by Lemma

. Combining (

) with (

)-(

Consider now

, the consistency result for

and

. The proof for

For the row-wise consistency note that we can use the decomposition in (

, then (

)-(

) follows analogously as

. Then

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\right\rVert&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

(43)

since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\right\rVert&=&\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{k_{2}}e_{tij}k^{-1}_{jj}c_{jq}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right]_{\cdot q}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\bar{c}C_{K}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}e_{tij}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemma
11
(ii),

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert&\lesssim&\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{tij}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
(i)
and Lemma
8
(iii),

\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\leq\frac{C_{K}}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{tij}\frac{\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}}\right)

follows directly from Assumption
(i)
. Iterating the same steps using Lemma

in place of Lemma

yields the result for
n>0
. Row-wise consistency for
\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}
can be established analogously.

Proof of Proposition
2
.

Since
\textrm{vec}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(n)}_{t}\right)=\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(n)}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right\rVert&\leq&\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{(n)}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right\rVert_{F}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\lVert\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\left\lVert\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|t}-\mathsf{f}^{LS(n)}_{t}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\mathsf{f}^{LS(n)}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
\end{array}

with
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}=\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}
and

\mathsf{f}^{LS(n)}_{t}=\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\right)\right)\mathsf{y}_{t}.

Consider the case
n=0
. Combining Lemmas D.15 and D.16 in
Barigozzi and Luciani 2024
with Lemmas
4
,
6
,
8
, we have that

\left\lVert\mathsf{f}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\mathsf{f}^{LS(0)}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)

Then,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\mathsf{f}^{LS(0)}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 108.405pt\left.\times\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\right)\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left.\hskip 72.26999pt-\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 36.135pt\times\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\otimes\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\otimes\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left.\hskip 72.26999pt\times\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&I+II+III+IV\end{array}

Now,

\begin{array}[]{rll}I&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iv),
3
(v),
4
(iii), and since
\left\lVert\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)
by Assumption
(ii)
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}II&\leq&\sqrt{p_{2}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 36.135pt\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\sqrt{p_{1}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 36.135pt\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&o_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iv),
3
(v),
4
(iii),
8
(ix),
8
(x), and Assumption
(ii)
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}III&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\lVert\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert_{F}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\right)\end{array}

by Lemma
8
(v),
8
(vi), and since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert^{2}_{F}\right]&=&\mathbb{E}\left[\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{1}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{k_{2}}\left(\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{\cdot i}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{c}_{\cdot j}\right)^{2}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{1}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{k_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}r_{si}h^{-1}_{ss}e_{tsq}k^{-1}_{qq}c_{qj}\right)^{2}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&k\bar{r}^{2}\bar{c}^{2}C^{2}_{K}C^{2}_{H}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{p_{2}}e_{tsq}\right)^{2}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\sum\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{q_{1},q_{2}}^{p_{2}}\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[e_{ts_{1}q_{1}}e_{ts_{2}q_{2}}\right]\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(p_{1}p_{2}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
(i)
,
(ii)
, and
(iii)
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}IV&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathsf{e}_{t}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}-\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemma
3
(v),
8
(iii)-
8
(vi) and since

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathsf{e}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}_{F}\right]=\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[|e_{tij}|^{2}\right]\leq p_{1}p_{2}C_{k}C_{h}=O(p_{1}p_{2})

by Assumption
(ii)
. Iterating the same steps, using Proposition
1
, Proposition (a.4)-(a.5) in
Barigozzi and Luciani 2024
, and Lemma
10
in place of Lemmas
4
,
6
,
8
, we can obtain the result for
n>0
.
∎

C.2
Auxiliary lemmata

C.2.1
Preliminary results

Lemma 1
.

Let
\mathbf{A}
and
\mathbf{B}
be
m\times n
and
mp\times nq
matrices, respectively. We have that

\left\lVert\mathbf{A}\star\mathbf{B}\right\rVert\leq mn\left\lVert\mathbf{A}\right\rVert_{\max}\left\lVert\mathbf{B}\right\rVert

Proof.

\begin{array}[]{rll}\lVert\mathbf{A}\star\mathbf{B}\lVert&=&\left\lVert\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}\mathbf{B}^{(p,q)}_{ij}\right\rVert\\
&\leq&\left\lVert\mathbf{A}\right\rVert_{\max}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n}\left\lVert\mathbf{B}^{(p,q)}_{ij}\right\rVert\\
&\leq&mn\left\lVert\mathbf{A}\right\rVert_{\max}\max\limits_{i,j}\left\lVert\mathbf{B}^{(p,q)}_{ij}\right\rVert\\
&\leq&mn\left\lVert\mathbf{A}\right\rVert_{\max}\left\lVert\mathbf{B}\right\rVert\\
\end{array}

∎

Lemma 2
.

For any
k_{1}\times k_{1}
and
k_{2}\times k_{2}
orthogonal matrices
\mathbf{J}_{1}
and
\mathbf{J}_{2}
, the DMFM in (
3
)-(
4
) is equivalent to

\displaystyle\mathbf{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}

(44)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\prime}+\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}

(45)

and its vectorized form is

\displaystyle\mathsf{y}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\otimes\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\right)\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}+\mathsf{e}_{t}

(46)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\otimes\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\right)\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}+\widetilde{\mathsf{u}}_{t}

(47)

with
\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime}_{t}\right]=\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}\textrm{tr}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}\right)
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}\right]=\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}\textrm{tr}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}\right)
such that
\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}=\widetilde{\bm{\Gamma}}^{P}\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}^{P}\widetilde{\bm{\Gamma}}^{P\prime}
and
\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}=\widetilde{\bm{\Gamma}}^{Q}\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}^{Q}\widetilde{\bm{\Gamma}}^{Q\prime}
, where

\begin{array}[]{lll}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}=\mathbf{R}\mathbf{J}_{1},&\qquad\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}=\mathbf{C}\mathbf{J}_{2},&\qquad\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime},\\
\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{J}_{1},&\qquad\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{J}_{2},&\qquad\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{U}_{t}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime}\\
\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{P}\mathbf{J}_{1},&\qquad\widetilde{\bm{\Gamma}}^{P}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\bm{\Gamma}^{P}&\qquad\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}^{P}=\bm{\Lambda}^{P}\\
\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{J}_{2}&\qquad\widetilde{\bm{\Gamma}}^{Q}=\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\bm{\Gamma}^{Q}&\qquad\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}^{Q}=\bm{\Lambda}^{Q}\\
\end{array}

Proof.

Plug-in all the rotated (“tilde”) matrices to obtain,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{Y}_{t}&=&\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\\
&=&\mathbf{R}\mathbf{J}_{1}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\\
&=&\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\\[18.06749pt]
\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}&=&\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\prime}+\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}\\
\mathbf{J}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}&=&\mathbf{J}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}^{\prime}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}+\mathbf{J}_{1}\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}\\
\mathbf{J}_{1}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}&=&\mathbf{J}_{1}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{J}_{1}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}+\mathbf{J}_{1}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{U}_{t}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{2}\\
\mathbf{F}_{t}&=&\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{B}^{\prime}+\mathbf{U}_{t}\end{array}

Moreover, using Assumption
(iv)
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rll}\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime}_{t}\right]&=&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{U}_{t}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\mathbf{U}_{t}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1})^{\prime}\right]\\
&=&\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{P}\textrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}\right)(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1})^{\prime}\\
&=&\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{P}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1})^{\prime}\textrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{Q}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2})^{\prime}\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\right)\\
&=&\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}\textrm{tr}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}\right)\end{array}

and analogous derivation can be obtained for
\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{t}\right]
. The derivation of the vectorized form follows naturally.
∎

Lemma 3
.

Consider the rotated system (
44
)-(
45
) defined in Lemma
2
. For any
k_{1}\times k_{1}
and
k_{2}\times k_{2}
orthogonal matrices
\mathbf{J}_{1}
and
\mathbf{J}_{2}
, under Assumptions
1
-
2
, we have that

(i)

\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\rVert\leq 1
,
\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\rVert<1
, and
\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\otimes\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\rVert<1

(ii)

\lVert{\mathbf{H}}\rVert=O(1)
,
\lVert{\mathbf{K}}\rVert=O(1)
, and
\lVert{\mathbf{K}}\otimes{\mathbf{H}}\rVert=O(1)

(iii)

p^{-1/2}_{1}\left\lVert{\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}}\right\rVert=O(1)
, and
p^{-1/2}_{2}\left\lVert{\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}}\right\rVert=O(1)
,

(iv)

p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O(1)
and
p_{1}\left\lVert\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O(1)

(v)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O(1)
and
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O(1)

Proof.

To show (i), note that by Assumption
(iii)
,

\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\rVert\leq\lVert\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{J}_{1}\rVert_{F}=\sqrt{\textrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{J}_{1}\mathbf{J}^{\prime}_{1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}(\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{1})^{\prime}\right)}=\sqrt{\textrm{tr}\left(\ \mathbf{A}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right)}=\lVert\mathbf{A}\rVert<1

\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\rVert=\lVert\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{J}_{2}\rVert<\lVert\mathbf{J}^{-1}_{2}\rVert\lVert\mathbf{B}\rVert\lVert\mathbf{J}_{2}\rVert=\lVert\mathbf{B}\rVert<1

and
\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\otimes\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\rVert\leq\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\rVert\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}\rVert<1
.
To show (ii), start noticing that since
\mathbf{H}
and
\mathbf{K}
are symmetric matrices then also
\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}
is symmetric, implying that
\lVert\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}\rVert=\rho(\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H})<\lVert\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}\rVert_{1}
. Note that each column of
\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}
can be written as
\mathbf{k}_{\cdot j}\otimes\mathbf{h}_{\cdot i}
for
j=1,\dots,p_{2}
and
i=1,\dots,p_{1}
. By the symmetry of
\mathbf{K}
and
\mathbf{H}
, we have that
\mathbf{k}_{\cdot j}\otimes\mathbf{h}_{\cdot i}=\mathbf{k}_{j\cdot}\otimes\mathbf{h}_{i\cdot}
and
\mathbf{k}_{j\cdot}\otimes\mathbf{h}_{i\cdot}=\textrm{vec}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{e}_{\cdot j}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\right]\right)
. We conclude
\lVert\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}\rVert_{1}=O(1)
by Assumption
(iii)
. The same conclusion follows for
\lVert{\mathbf{H}}\rVert
and
\lVert{\mathbf{K}}\rVert
noticing that
\lVert\mathbf{K}\otimes\mathbf{H}\rVert=\lVert\mathbf{K}\rVert\lVert\mathbf{H}\rVert
.
To show (iii), note that by Assumption
(i)

\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\right\rVert^{2}\asymp\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\right\rVert^{2}_{F}=\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{k_{1}}r^{2}_{ij}\leq k_{1}\bar{r}=O(1),

and

\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right\rVert^{2}\asymp\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\right\rVert^{2}_{F}=\frac{1}{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{k_{2}}c^{2}_{ij}\leq k_{2}\bar{c}=O(1).

To show (iv), note that by Theorem 1 in
Merikoski and Kumar 2004
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rll}p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert&=&\frac{p_{2}}{\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right)}\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\frac{p_{2}}{\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right)\nu^{(p_{2})}\left(\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right)}\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\frac{p_{2}}{\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right)\left(\nu^{(1)}\left(\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)\right)\right)^{-1}}\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{1}{C^{-1}_{K}}\\[7.22743pt]
\end{array}

by Assumptions
(i)
and
(ii)
. The proof for
p_{1}\left\lVert\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
follows the same steps.
To show (v), note that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert&\leq&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}\right\rVert\max\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}k^{-1}_{ii}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O(1)\end{array}

by (iii) and Assumption
(ii)
. The proof for
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert
follows the same steps.
∎

C.2.2
Results on pre-estimators

Lemma 4
.

Under Assumptions
1
through
3
, there exist matrices
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}
satisfying
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}k_{1}}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}k_{2}}
, such that as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
,

(i)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right\}\right)

(ii)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)

(iii)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

Proof.

Note that (i) and (ii) follows immediately from Theorem 3.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
. Moreover, consider (iii) and note that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert&\leq&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\otimes\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\otimes\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}_{2}}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right\}\right)+O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by (i), (ii) and Lemma
3
(iii).
∎

Lemma 5
.

Under Assumptions
1
through
3
, there exist matrices
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}
satisfying
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}k_{1}}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}k_{2}}
, such that for
s_{1},s_{2}\in\{0,1\}
as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
,

(i)

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t}^{T}\left(\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{y}_{t-s_{1}}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s_{1}}\right)\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-s_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1\prime}=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)
,

(ii)

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t}^{T}\left(\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{y}_{t-s_{1}}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s_{1}}\right)\mathsf{u}^{\prime}_{t}=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right),

with
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}=\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}
.

Proof.

Note that the left hand side of (i) can be written as

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left(\textrm{vec}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}}{p_{1}}\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t-s_{1}}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1\prime}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}+\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}}{p_{1}}\mathbf{E}_{t}\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\right)-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s_{1}}\right)\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-s_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1\prime}

(48)

By Theorem 3.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
, we have that

p^{-1}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\mathbf{R}=\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}+o_{p}(1),\qquad p^{-1}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\mathbf{C}=\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}+o_{p}(1),

(49)

therefore (
48
) is asymptotically equivalent to

\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}_{t=2}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{e}_{t-s_{1}}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-s_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1\prime}.

(50)

We can bound (
50
) as follows

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=2}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1\prime}&\lesssim&\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=2}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}-\frac{\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\otimes\frac{\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1\prime}\\
&&+\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\otimes\frac{\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1\prime}\\
&\lesssim&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right).\end{array}

by Assumption
(i)
, and Lemmas
3
and
4
. Consider (ii) and note that by (
49
), we have

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t}^{T}\left(\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{y}_{t-s_{1}}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s_{1}}\right)\mathsf{u}^{\prime}_{t}&\lesssim&\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=2}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{u}^{\prime}_{t}.\\
&\lesssim&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right).\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
and
4
, and since
We can bound (
50
) as follows

\begin{array}[]{rll}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\sum\limits^{T}_{t=2}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{u}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right]&\leq&\frac{1}{T^{2}p_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{j_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\sum\limits_{j_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\sum\limits^{T}_{t,s=1}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{ti_{1}i_{2}}e_{si_{1}i_{2}}u_{tj_{1}j_{2}}u_{sj_{1}j_{2}}\right]\\
&\leq&\frac{kC_{P}C_{Q}}{T^{2}p_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{i_{1}=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits^{T}_{t,s=1}\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[e_{ti_{1}i_{2}}e_{si_{1}i_{2}}\right]\right\rvert\\
&\lesssim&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{T}\right).\end{array}

by Assumptions
(iv)
and
(iii)
.
∎

Lemma 6
.

Under Assumptions
1
through
3
, there exist matrices
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}
satisfying
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}
, such that as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
,

(i)

\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}(\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A})\widehat{\mathbf{J}}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(ii)

\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}(\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P})\widehat{\mathbf{J}}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)
,

with
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}=\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}
.

Proof.

Consider (i) and note that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}(\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A})\widehat{\mathbf{J}}\right\rVert&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}-\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}\right)\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[14.45377pt]
&&+\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}(\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A})\widehat{\mathbf{J}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}-\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}\right)\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[14.45377pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\mathsf{u}_{t}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert.\end{array}

where
\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}=\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}}{p_{2}}\otimes\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}}{p_{1}}\right)^{\prime}\mathsf{y}_{t}
. Now, for
s=\{0,1\}
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-s}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}-\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}&=&\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-s}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s}\right)\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}+\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s}\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-s}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-s}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}.\end{array}

Note that the first two terms dominate the third one. By Lemma
5
(i) we have that

\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}-\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}-\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right).

Moreover, combining the latter with Assumption
(ii)
, we have that,

\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right)^{-1}=O_{p}(1).

Finally, note that

\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\mathsf{u}_{t}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}^{\prime}_{t-1}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\mathsf{u}_{t}\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}-\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)\right\rVert+\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\mathsf{u}_{t}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\min\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right),

by Lemma
5
(ii) and Assumption
(iv)
, concluding the proof. The result for (ii) follows using the same steps of (i) noting that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}&=&\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left\{\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\right\}\left\{\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}\right\}^{\prime}\\[14.45377pt]
&=&\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=2}^{T}\left\{\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)\right.\\[14.45377pt]
&&\hskip 35.56593pt\left.+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\right)\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}+\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)+\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{u}_{t}\right\}\\[14.45377pt]
&&\hskip 28.45274pt\left\{\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\right)\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)\right.\\[14.45377pt]
&&\hskip 35.56593pt\left.+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\right)\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}+\mathbf{B}\otimes\mathbf{A}\left(\widetilde{\mathsf{f}}_{t-1}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t-1}\right)+\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{u}_{t}\right\}^{\prime}\end{array}

and that

\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{u}_{t}\mathsf{u}^{\prime}_{t}-\mathbf{Q}\otimes\mathbf{P}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)

by Assumption
(iv)
.
∎

Lemma 7
.

Under Assumption (
1
) through (
3
), as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
,

(i)

\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}},\right\}\right)
uniformly in
j

(ii)

\left\lvert\widehat{h}^{(0)}_{ii}-h_{ii}\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right\}\right)
uniformly in
i

(iii)

\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right)\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)

(iv)

\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\widehat{h}^{(0)}_{ii}-h_{ii}\right)\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right\}\right)

Proof.

Start from (i), and recall by Assumption
(ii)
we have that
\textrm{tr}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)=p_{1}
, thus
\mathbf{K}=\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{E}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right]
. We can then write

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert&=&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}(\widehat{e}^{(0)}_{tij})^{2}-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left((s_{tij}-\widehat{s}^{(0)}_{tij})^{2}+2(s_{tij}-\widehat{s}^{(0)}_{tij})e_{tij}+e^{2}_{tij}\right)-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}(s_{tij}-\widehat{s}^{(0)}_{tij})^{2}\right\rvert+2\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}(s_{tij}-\widehat{s}^{(0)}_{tij})e_{tij}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}e^{2}_{tij}-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\rvert\end{array}

Consider the first addendum, we have

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}(s_{tij}-\widehat{s}^{(0)}_{tij})^{2}\right\rvert&=&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{s}_{t\cdot j}-\widehat{\mathbf{s}}^{(0)}_{t\cdot j})^{2}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

(51)

since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\right\rvert&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}}{\mathbf{F}_{t}}\right\rVert\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{2}},\frac{1}{p^{2}_{1}p^{2}_{2}},\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}_{1}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

because of Lemma
4
(i) and Assumption
(ii)

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\right\rvert&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\times\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rVert\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{1}p_{2}}\right)\end{array}

because of Lemma E.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
and since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{2}&=&\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{1}}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}+\frac{1}{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\mathbf{C}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\end{array}

(52)

we have that

\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rVert\lesssim\frac{1}{Tp_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}}\right)

(53)

by Lemma A.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
and Chebyshev inequality,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rvert&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}}{\mathbf{F}_{t}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\frac{1}{Tp_{2}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}}\right)O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right)\end{array}

because of Theorem 3.1 and Lemma E.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
and Assumption
(ii)
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\right\rvert&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp^{2}_{1}p^{2}_{2}}\sum^{T}_{t=1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\frac{1}{Tp^{2}_{1}p^{2}_{2}}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathbf{R}\right\rVert\sum_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\lVert\sum^{T}_{t=1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{ti\cdot}\mathbf{E}^{\prime}_{t\cdot j}\mathbf{R}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

because of (
52
) and (A.3) in
Yu et al. 2022
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rvert&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}}\right)O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

because of (
53
) and Theorem 3.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{F}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right\rvert&\lesssim&\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&o_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}},\frac{1}{p^{2}_{1}p^{2}_{2}},\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

because of Assumption
(ii)
and Theorem 3.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
. Consider now the second addendum,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}(s_{tij}-\widehat{s}^{(0)}_{tij})e_{tij}\right\rvert&=&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{s}_{t\cdot j}-\widehat{\mathbf{s}}^{(0)}_{t\cdot j})\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}p_{1}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)\end{array}

(54)

since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rvert&\lesssim&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}p_{1}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by the same steps as in the proof of Lemma B.3 in
Yu et al. 2022
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rvert&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp^{2}_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp^{2}_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}^{\prime}_{t}\right]_{.i}\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t.i}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{t\cdot j}\mathbf{R}\right\rVert\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by (A.3) in
Yu et al. 2022
, and

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rvert&\leq&\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\rVert\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}}\right)\end{array}

by Theorem 3.1 and assumption
(i)
. Finally, note that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}e^{2}_{tij}-\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right)^{2}\right]&=&\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}_{1}}\sum\limits_{t,s}^{T}\sum\limits_{i_{1},i_{2}}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(e^{2}_{ti_{1}j}-\mathbb{E}[e^{2}_{ti_{1}j}]\right)\left(e^{2}_{si_{2}j}-\mathbb{E}[e^{2}_{si_{2}j}]\right)\right]\\[7.22743pt]

&=&O\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right)\end{array}

(55)

by Assumption

. Therefore, for the third addendum, we have

\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}e^{2}_{tij}-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}}\right)

Consider (iii), we have that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right)\right\rvert&=&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}(\widehat{e}^{(0)}_{tij})^{2}-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]

Consider the first addendum, we have that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}(s_{tij}-\widehat{s}^{(0)}_{tij})^{2}\right\}\right\rvert&=&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{s}_{t\cdot j}-\widehat{\mathbf{s}}^{(0)}_{t\cdot j})^{\prime}(\mathbf{s}_{t\cdot j}-\widehat{\mathbf{s}}^{(0)}_{t\cdot j})\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]

\begin{array}[]{l}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
