&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}p_{1}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

(58)

since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\}\right\rvert&=&\textrm{tr}\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}p_{1}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemma
3
(iii) and (B.3) in
Yu et al. 2022
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{j\cdot}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\}\right\rvert&=&\textrm{tr}\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp^{2}_{1}p^{2}_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by (
52
), Lemma
3
(iii) and (A.3) in
Yu et al. 2022
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{c}_{j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot j}\right\}\right\rvert&=&\textrm{tr}\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{1}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}\sqrt{p_{2}}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}}\right)\end{array}

by Lemma
4
(ii) and A.1 in
Yu et al. 2022
. Consider the third addendum, since the result in (
55
) does not depend on
j
, we have that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}e^{2}_{tij}-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\}\right\rvert&=&\max\limits_{j}\left\lvert\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}e^{2}_{tij}-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}}\right).\end{array}

(59)

Combining (
57
), (
58
) and (
59
) yields the desired result. The proof of (iv) follows similar steps.
∎

Lemma 8
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
2
, we have that as
\min\left\{T,p_{1},p_{2}\right\}\rightarrow\infty

(i)

\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(ii)

\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(iii)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)

(iv)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}-\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right\}\right)

(v)

p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(vi)

p_{1}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(vii)

p_{2}
\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)

(viii)

p_{1}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right\}\right)

(ix)

\sqrt{p_{2}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)

(x)

\sqrt{p_{1}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\right\}\right)

Proof.

Consider (i) and note that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right\rVert&=&\left\{\nu^{(p_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)}\right)\right\}^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\{\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}k_{jj}+\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\}^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\{\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}k_{jj}-\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert\right\}^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\{C^{-1}_{K}-\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert\right\}^{-1}=C_{K}+O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
(ii)
and Lemma
7
(i). The proof for (ii) follows the same steps. Consider (iii), we have that

\begin{array}[]{l}p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}=p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{i=j}^{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}_{j\cdot}\widehat{k}^{(0)-1}_{jj}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}{k}^{-1}_{jj}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
=p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}_{j\cdot}\left(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}+k_{jj}\right)^{-1}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}{k}^{-1}_{jj}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
=p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}_{j\cdot}k^{-1}_{jj}\left(1+(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj})/k_{jj}\right)^{-1}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}{k}^{-1}_{jj}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
\leq p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\left(1+\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj})/k_{jj}\right)^{-1}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}_{j\cdot}k^{-1}_{jj}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}{k}^{-1}_{jj}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
\leq p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\left(1-\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj})/k_{jj}+o\left(\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj})/k_{jj}\right)\right)\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}_{j\cdot}k^{-1}_{jj}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}{\mathbf{c}}_{j\cdot}{k}^{-1}_{jj}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
\leq p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}+p_{2}^{-1}\left(\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}\left\lvert(\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj})/k_{jj}\right\rvert\right)^{2}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
\leq p_{2}^{-1}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}+p_{2}^{-1}C^{2}_{K}\left(\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert\right)^{2}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
\qquad+p_{2}^{-1}C^{2}_{K}\left(\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(0)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert\right)^{2}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}\\[7.22743pt]
=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{Tp_{1}},\frac{1}{p^{2}_{1}p^{2}_{2}},\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iii),
4
,
7
(iii) and Assumption
(ii)
. Part (iv) follows analogously.
Consider (v) and note that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\det\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}&=&\prod\limits_{j=1}^{k_{2}}\nu^{(j)}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&\geq&\left(\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)\right)^{k_{2}}\\[7.22743pt]
&\geq&\left(\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)-\left|\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)-\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\right|\right)^{k_{2}}\end{array}

From Lemma
3
(iv), we have that
\lim\limits_{p_{2}\rightarrow\infty}p^{-1}_{2}\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)>0
. Moreover,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left|\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)-\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\right|&\leq&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iii),
3
(v), Proposition
1
and term (iii), implyingthat
\det\left(p^{-1}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}>0
with probability tending to one as
\min\{T,p_{1},p_{2}\}
goes to infinity, i.e.
p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)
. The proof for (vi) follows the same steps.
Consider (vii), we have that

\begin{array}[]{rll}p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert&\leq&p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

because of Lemma
3
(iv), term (v) and since
\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right\}\right)
by the same steps used in the proof of term (iii). Proof for (viii) follows analogously. The proofs for (ix) and (x) follow directly from (iii) and (vii), and from (iv) and (viii), respectively.
∎

C.2.3
Results on EM estimators

Lemma 9
.

Under Assumption (
1
) through (
3
), for all
n\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
,

(i)

\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)
uniformly in
j

(ii)

\left\lvert\widehat{h}^{(n)}_{ii}-h_{ii}\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)
uniformly in
i

(iii)

\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right)\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(iv)

\frac{1}{p_{1}}\left\lvert\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\left(\widehat{h}^{(n)}_{ii}-h_{ii}\right)\right\rvert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

Proof.

Consider (ii) and recall that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\widehat{h}^{(n)}_{ii}&=&\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{Y}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n-1)-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}-\mathbf{Y}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n-1)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\mathbf{F}^{(n-1)\prime}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\mathbf{F}^{(n-1)}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n-1)-1}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}_{t}\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n-1)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n-1)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n-1)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(n-1)}_{t|T}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\right)^{\prime}\right)\right]_{ii},\end{array}

and that
h_{ii}=\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}\right]
. Let
n=1
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lvert\widehat{h}^{(1)}_{ii}-h_{ii}\right\rvert&=&\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{r}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)}\mathbf{F}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{r}}^{(1)}_{i\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{r}}^{(1)}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)\prime}\mathbf{F}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{r}}^{(1)}_{i\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)}\right)\star\left(\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(1)}\right)\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}\mathsf{f}^{(n)\prime}_{t|T}+\mathbf{\Pi}^{(0)}_{t|T}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}\right)\right]_{ii}\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 108.405pt\left.-\widehat{\mathbf{r}}^{(1)}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{r}_{i\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}-\frac{1}{p_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{ti\cdot}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}\right]\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&I+II+III+IV+V\end{array}

For term
I
we have that

\begin{array}[]{l}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{r}_{i\cdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)}\mathbf{F}^{(0)\prime}_{t|T}\widehat{\mathbf{r}}^{(0)}_{i\cdot}\right)\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 72.26999pt\leq\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\mathbf{r}_{i\cdot}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
\hskip 72.26999pt+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(1)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}_{1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-\prime}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{r}}^{(1)}_{i\cdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\mathbf{r}_{i\cdot}\right)\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]

&&+\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{tij}-\mathbb{E}\left[e^{2}_{tij}\right]\right\rvert\left\lvert k^{-1}_{jj}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions

,
(iv)
and Lemma

(iii). The proof for (i) follows the same steps. Consider (iv), then

Lemma 10
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
2
, for all
n\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, we have that as
\min\left\{T,p_{1},p_{2}\right\}\rightarrow\infty

(i)

\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(ii)

\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(iii)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(iv)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}-\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right))

(v)

p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(vi)

p_{1}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

(vii)

p_{2}
\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(viii)

p_{1}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(ix)

\sqrt{p_{2}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(x)

\sqrt{p_{1}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}-\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

Proof.

Consider (i) and note that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\right\rVert&=&\left\{\nu^{(p_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)}\right)\right\}^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\{\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}k_{jj}+\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\}^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\{\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}k_{jj}-\min\limits_{j=1,\dots,p_{2}}\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert\right\}^{-1}\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\left\{C^{-1}_{K}-\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert\right\}^{-1}=C_{K}+O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
(ii)
and Lemma
9
(i). The proof for (ii) follows the same steps. Consider (iii), we have that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert&\lesssim&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right)\right\rVert\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

because of Proposition
1
, Assumption
(ii)
, and since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right)\right\rVert&\leq&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\left\{\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left\lvert\left[\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right)\right]_{ij}\right\rvert^{2}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\sqrt{k_{2}}\max\limits_{ij}\left\lvert\mathbf{c}^{\prime}_{\cdot i}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right]_{\cdot j}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\bar{c}\sqrt{k_{2}}\max\limits_{j}\left\lvert\left\{\widehat{k}^{(n)-1}_{jj}k^{-1}_{jj}\left(\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right)\right\}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\bar{c}\sqrt{k_{2}}\max\limits_{j}\left\lvert\left(\min\limits_{j}\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}\right)^{-1}\left(\min\limits_{j}k_{jj}\right)^{-1}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\left(\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right)\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\bar{c}\sqrt{k_{2}}C^{2}_{K}\left\lvert\widehat{k}^{(n)}_{jj}-k_{jj}\right\rvert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}},\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
(i)
,
(ii)
and Lemma
9
(i). The proof for (iv) follows the same steps.
Consider (v) and note that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\det\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}&=&\prod\limits_{j=1}^{k_{2}}\nu^{(j)}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\\[7.22743pt]
&\geq&\left(\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)\right)^{k_{2}}\\[7.22743pt]
&\geq&\left(\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)-\left|\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)-\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\right|\right)^{k_{2}}\end{array}

From Lemma
3
(iv), we have that
\lim\limits_{p_{2}\rightarrow\infty}p^{-1}_{2}\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)>0
. Moreover,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left|\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)-\nu^{(k_{2})}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)\right|&\leq&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}-\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iii),
3
(v), Proposition
1
and term (iii), implying that
\det\left(p^{-1}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}>0
with probability tending to one as
\min\{T,p_{1},p_{2}\}
goes to infinity, i.e.
p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)
. The proof for (vi) follows the same steps.
Consider (vii), we have that

\begin{array}[]{rll}p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert&\leq&p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert p_{2}\left\lVert\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}}\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

because of Lemma
3
(iv), term (v) and since
\frac{1}{p_{2}}\left\lVert\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}-\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}}\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)
. Proof for (viii) follows analogously. The proofs for (ix) and (x) follow directly from (iii) and (vii), and from (iv) and (viii), respectively.
∎

Lemma 11
.

Under Assumptions
1
through
3
, there exist matrices
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}
satisfying
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{1}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{1}k_{1}}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{\prime}_{2}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbb{I}_{k_{2}k_{2}}
, such that, for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
, as
\min\left\{p_{1},p_{2},T\right\}\rightarrow\infty
,

(i)

\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{J}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(ii)

\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}e_{tij}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(iii)

\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}e_{tij}(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t})\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(iv)

\left\lVert\frac{1}{T\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathbf{e}^{\prime}_{t\cdot i}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

(v)

\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{1}\sqrt{p_{2}}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t})^{\prime}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)

Proof.

Let
\mathsf{x}_{t}=\left\{\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{J}},\frac{1}{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{tij},\frac{1}{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}e_{tij},\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}}\sum\limits^{p_{2}}_{i=1}\mathbf{e}_{t\cdot i},\frac{1}{p_{1}\sqrt{p_{2}}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p_{1}}\mathbf{e}_{ti\cdot}\right\}
. From (B.4) in
Barigozzi and Luciani 2024
, we have that

\begin{array}[]{rll}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert&\leq&\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|T}-\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|t}\right)\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{(n)}_{t|t}-\mathsf{f}^{LS(n)}_{t}\right)\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathsf{f}^{LS(n)}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\right)\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&I+II+III\end{array}

where

\mathsf{f}^{LS(n)}_{t}=\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(n)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(n)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(n)-1}\right)\right)\mathsf{y}_{t}

Consider the case
n=0
. From Lemmas
4
,
6
,
8
, (B.5) and (B.6) in
Barigozzi and Luciani 2024
, it follows that terms I and II are both
O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right)
. Focusing on the third term, we have

\begin{array}[]{rll}III&\leq&\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 108.405pt\left.\times\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\left(\left({\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right)\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 108.405pt\left.\times\left(\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left.\hskip 108.405pt-\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 36.135pt\times\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\left\lVert\left(\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\right)\right)\right.\\[7.22743pt]
&&\left.\hskip 108.405pt-\left(\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&=III_{a}+III_{b}+III_{c}+III_{d}\end{array}

Since,

\begin{array}[]{rll}III_{a}&\leq&\left\lVert p_{2}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert p_{1}\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 36.135pt\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iv),
3
(v),
4
(iii),

\begin{array}[]{rll}III_{b}&\lesssim&\sqrt{p_{2}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 72.26999pt\times\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\sqrt{p_{1}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 72.26999pt\times\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{\mathbf{C}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{R}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}_{1}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{R}}}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&o_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iv),
3
(v),
4
(iii),
8
(ix) and
8
(x),

\begin{array}[]{rll}III_{c}&\leq&\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\mathbf{R}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&\lesssim&\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\end{array}

by Lemma
3
(iv), and

\begin{array}[]{rll}III_{d}&\lesssim&\sqrt{p_{2}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{K}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 72.26999pt\times\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}}{p_{1}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\sqrt{p_{1}}\left\lVert\left(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{(0)-1}-\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&&\hskip 72.26999pt\times\left\lVert\left(\frac{\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{C}}{p_{2}}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert\frac{\left\lVert\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathrm{dg}\left(\mathbf{K}\right)^{-1}\right\rVert}{\sqrt{p_{1}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\end{array}

by Lemmas
3
(iv)-(v),
4
,
8
(ix)-(x), we obtain

\begin{array}[]{rll}III&=&O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}},\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\right\}\right)\left\{\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\right\}\\[7.22743pt]
&&+\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{x}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert\end{array}

From Lemma
2
, we have that the stochastic behavior of
\widehat{\mathbf{J}}^{-1}\mathsf{f}_{t}
is equivalent to that of
\mathsf{f}_{t}
. Set
\mathsf{x}_{t}=\mathsf{f}_{t}
, we have that

\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{f}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

by Assumption
(ii)
,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)

by Assumption
(i)

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}\mathsf{f}^{\prime}_{t}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
(i)
and Lemma
3
(iii). This concludes the proof for (i). Let
\mathsf{x}_{t}=\frac{1}{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}e_{tij}
, we have that

\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{f}_{t}e_{tij}\right\rVert=O_{p}(1)

by Assumption
(i)
,

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum^{p_{2}}\limits_{i=1}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{e}_{t}e_{tij}\right\rVert&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{2}}}\right)\end{array}

since

\begin{array}[]{rll}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{{p_{1}p_{2}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum^{p_{2}}\limits_{i=1}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}\mathsf{e}_{t}e_{tij}\right\rVert^{2}_{F}\right]&=&\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{{p_{1}p_{2}}}\sum\limits_{l}^{p_{1}}\sum\limits_{h}^{p_{2}}\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}e_{tlh}e_{tij}\right\rvert^{2}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\max\limits_{l}\max\limits_{h}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lvert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum^{T}\limits_{t=1}e_{tlh}e_{tij}\right\rvert^{2}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&\max\limits_{l}\max\limits_{h}\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}_{2}}\sum\limits_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{p_{2}}\sum^{T}\limits_{s,t=1}\mathbb{E}\left[e_{tlh}e_{tij_{1}}e_{slh}e_{sij_{2}}\right]\\[7.22743pt]
&=&O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
(iv)
, and

\begin{array}[]{rll}\frac{1}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{Tp_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p_{2}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\prime}\mathbf{K}^{-1}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\right)\mathsf{e}_{t}e_{tij}\right\rVert=O_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{Tp_{1}p_{2}}}\right\}\right)\end{array}

This concludes the proof for (ii). The results for (iii), (iv) and (v) can be established analogously. Repeating the same steps for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
using Proposition
1
, Proposition 1 (a.4)-(a.5) in
Barigozzi and Luciani 2024
, and Lemma
10
in place of Lemmas
4
,
6
,
8
completes the proof. ∎

Appendix D
Cointegrated factors and common trends

To prove (
18
), we must find a
k_{1}\times k_{1}
invertible matrix
\bm{\mathcal{R}}
and a
k_{2}\times k_{2}
invertible matrix
\bm{\mathcal{C}}
such that

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{R}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{G}_{1t}&\mathbf{0}_{r_{1},q_{2}}\\
\mathbf{0}_{q_{1},r_{2}}&\mathbf{G}_{0t}\end{array}\right),\quad\mathbf{R}\bm{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}=\left[\mathbf{R}_{1}\;\;\mathbf{R}_{0}\right],\quad\mathbf{C}\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{-1}=\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}\;\;\mathbf{C}_{0}\right].

Here, as an illustration, we provide one possible choice.
Let
\bm{\beta}_{1}
be
k_{1}\times q_{1}
such that
\bm{\beta}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{1}=\mathbb{I}_{q_{1}}
and
\text{vec}(\bm{\beta}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})\sim I(0)
, which means that all columns of
\mathbf{F}_{t}
have the same cointegration relations. Similarly, let
\bm{\beta}_{2}
be
k_{2}\times q_{2}
such that
\bm{\beta}_{2}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{2}=\mathbb{I}_{q_{2}}
and
\text{vec}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{2})\sim I(0)
, which means that all rows of
\mathbf{F}_{t}
have the same cointegration relations. Let also
\bm{\beta}_{i\perp}
be
k_{i}\times k_{i}-q_{i}
such that

\bm{\beta}_{i\perp}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{i\perp}=\mathbb{I}_{k_{i}-q_{i}}
and
\bm{\beta}_{i\perp}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{i}=\mathbf{0}_{k_{i}-q_{i},q_{i}}
, for
i=1,2
. Let us also assume that
\bm{\beta}_{i\perp}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{j}=\mathbf{0}_{k_{i}-q_{i},q_{j}}
for
i\neq j
. Then,

\bm{\mathcal{R}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\beta}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\bm{\beta}_{1\perp}^{\prime}\\
\end{array}\right)\quad\text{and}\quad\bm{\mathcal{C}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\beta}_{2}^{\prime}\\
\bm{\beta}_{2\perp}^{\prime}\\
\end{array}\right).

\mathbf{A}
and
\mathbf{B}

,

, and the innovation covariance matrices

B.2
. Table
4

E.2
Handling missing data: Initialization from balanced subpanels

As an alternative initialization strategy for datasets with missing observations, we consider using starting values derived by applying our EM algorithm to a fully observed subset of the original matrix
\mathbf{Y}_{t}
. Because this approach necessitates excluding any rows and columns with missing values, we focus on the block missing data pattern. For comparison, we continue to use the PE estimator as a benchmark, applied to the original matrix after imputation using the method proposed by
Cen and Lam 2025
. Table
5
reports summary statistics for the ratio of the EM estimator’s performance relative to that of the PE. The results further confirm that the EM algorithm yields improved estimates compared to the PE.
